| **DECISION-110** | Deflated Sharpe (Bailey et al.) — Pass 52 owner-approved. PSR formula in metrics.py: `PSR = Φ((SR - SR*) × sqrt((n-1) / (1 - γ3·SR + ((γ4-1)/4)·SR²)))`. SR* ≈ √(2·ln(72)) ≈ 2.92 for our 72-strategy universe. Threshold PSR ≥ 0.95 = pass; below = noise-dominated. Joint with DEC-080 (Bonferroni), DEC-081 (Sharpe canonicalization), DEC-246 (quant audit). | **DECISION-131** | Agent value-add minimum (Sharpe improvement ≥0.2 over rules-only) — Pass 52 owner-approved with two-gate refinement (Pass 52 turn 13). **Two-gate logic:** primary gate `agent_sharpe - rules_sharpe ≥ 0.2` (absolute); secondary gate `(agent_sharpe - rules_sharpe) / max(rules_sharpe, 0.1) ≥ 0.15` (relative); pass if EITHER gate clears + Bonferroni p<0.05 (joint with DEC-080). Catches both failure modes per CAV-060: low-baseline trap (+0.2 absolute is huge proportionally) and high-baseline trap (+0.2 absolute is noise). A/B comparison: Stage 2 candidates run twice (rules-only + agent-overlay) on same trades. Failure mode: abandon Stage 2 agent overlay per PROJECT_PLAN section 4. Cost-justification: $300 cap ÷ Sharpe improvement gates Stage 3+ adoption. Joint with DEC-205-209 (RESOLVED A/B framework), DEC-080 (Bonferroni). | **DECISION-261** | ICT/SMC PIT rules — Pass 52 owner-approved with N+1 lag rule. Pattern completion lag: ICT/SMC patterns complete on bar N (e.g., 3-bar FVG forms at N-2/N-1/N); actionable signal is bar N+1 open at earliest. Rationale: at end-of-day N, pattern is "known" but no further trading possible same day for daily bars; earliest tradable execution is bar N+1 open. For intra-bar events (BOS, CHoCH, liquidity grab): same N+1 rule — events recorded at bar N (close confirms event) but actionable from bar N+1 open. Joint with DEC-040 PIT loader + DEC-259 cache schema (lag computed at consumption time, not stored as offset in cache). **Use-case map (per #57):** ICT/SMC strategies (DEC-259 consumers), PIT loader uniform lag application, backtest correctness. ~0.5 days rule documentation + 2-line check in strategy entry logic. **Test signals:** synthetic FVG forms at bar 100 → strategy entry at bar 101 open (not bar 100); zero-lag entry triggers test failure flag. **Pass 52 turn 77 owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED (bookkeeping flip per status drift): ICT/SMC PIT rules with N+1 lag rule. Pattern completion lag: ICT/SMC patterns complete on bar N; actionable signal is bar N+1 open at earliest. Pattern "known" at end-of-day N but no same-day trading for daily bars. For intra-bar events (BOS, CHoCH, liquidity grab): same N+1 rule. Sprint 1 work item per ENGINEERING_REGISTER. Joint with DEC-259 (ICT/SMC cache uses this rule). PIT correctness foundational — without N+1 lag, ICT signals would have lookahead bias. ~0.5d (documentation rule + enforcement test). Test signal: synthetic FVG forms at bar 100 → strategy entry triggers at bar 101 open (not bar 100 close).** | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Batch X41 — Phase 0.A Data Prefetch Gaps | Pass 47 | - |

For each closed trade, decompose into 5 components: (1) Signal contribution — P&L if exited at idealized signal-driven point; (2) Timing contribution — P&L delta from actual entry timing vs idealized; (3) Exit contribution — P&L delta from actual exit vs idealized; (4) Sizing contribution — P&L delta from actual size vs equal-weight baseline; (5) Agent contribution — P&L delta when agent overlay differs from rules baseline (per DEC-210). | **DECISION-420** | DEC-131 implementation — Agent value-add A/B framework with three-case pairing (Pass 52 turn 13 owner-approved). Run Stage 2 candidates twice: rules-only path + agent-overlay path. Per (ticker, date, signal) tuple, compute one of three cases: (1) rules=YES, agent=YES → `paired_pnl = agent_pnl - rules_pnl`; (2) rules=YES, agent=NO → `counter_pnl = -rules_pnl` (agent disagreement saves/misses rules outcome); (3) rules=NO, agent=YES → `counter_pnl = +agent_pnl` (agent-originated trade); (4) rules=NO, agent=NO → no observation (both correctly skip; not counted). Aggregate: `agent_value_add = sum(paired_pnl) + sum(counter_pnl)`. Compute `agent_sharpe` and `rules_sharpe` from full trade lists; apply DEC-131 two-gate test. Three-case logic prevents bias from disagreement under-counting. ~3 days (was ~2 days; +1 day for three-case correctness). Joint with DEC-205-209 (A/B infrastructure). **Test signals:** rules_yes_agent_no with rules_pnl<0 → counter_pnl>0 (agent saved loss); rules_no_agent_yes with agent_pnl>0 → counter_pnl>0 (agent originated win); paired case with agent_pnl>rules_pnl → paired_pnl>0. **Pass 52 turn 119 RESOLVED-DECIDED Final: DEC-131 implementation — Agent value-add A/B framework three-case pairing. hold duration EXCLUDED — it's an outcome not input):** Strategy (dynamic count, ~60 + DEC-355-362 chart patterns + future additions), Exit method (17 after DEC-067), Regime (4), Sector (11 GICS), Confidence tier (4), Direction (2), Days-to-earnings (4 buckets), Smart money score (3 bands), VIX level (4 bands finer than regime), Yield curve state (3), AAII extreme (3), CNN F&G (5), Cap-band (4), Volatility-bucket (4), Distance from 52wk high (3), Days-to-FOMC/CPI/NFP (3 each), Earnings season binary, Market breadth (3), Relative strength vs sector (3). **Per-cell output schema (REVISED Pass 52 turn 6 per owner correction — original schema omitted ROI/R:R/profit factor):** Performance (`roi_pct`, `sharpe`, `sortino`); R:R + trade quality (`reward_risk_ratio`, `rr_compliance` boolean per DEC-353 2R minimum, `profit_factor`, `expectancy`); Sample (`win_rate`, `n_trades`, `avg_win`, `avg_loss`, `avg_hold_days` as outcome diagnostic); Drawdown/capacity (`max_drawdown`, `calmar`, `max_adverse_excursion_avg`, `max_favourable_excursion_avg`); Cost-adjusted (`roi_after_costs`, `sharpe_at_5bps/10bps/20bps` per DEC-081 phase C); Statistical confidence (`bonferroni_p`, `psr` per DEC-110, `t_stat`, `ci_95`); Fallback metadata (`fallback_used`, `fallback_reason`); **Pair verdict (PASS | FAIL_RR | FAIL_CONFIDENCE | FAIL_DRAWDOWN | INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE) — FAIL_RR is HARD REJECT regardless of other metrics per DEC-353 2R constraint.** **Methodology:** Hybrid Approach A (marginal heatmaps for stat validity) + Approach B (3D combined for top-N strategies); Approach C (Bayesian shrinkage) deferred to Phase D. **Statistical gates per cell:** n_trades ≥ 30 + Bonferroni p<0.05 + PSR ≥ 0.95 + t-stat ≥ 3.4 + **R:R ≥ 2.0 (DEC-353 hard constraint)**. **Implementation effort:** ~20-28 days (4-6 weeks). **Pre-condition gate before Stage 1 backtest run.** **Test signals (per DEC-417/CHECKLIST #54):** test run produces non-empty cube; cost-adjusted Sharpe at 5/10/20bps differs from base Sharpe (verifies cost sensitivity scaffolding wired). | **DECISION-458** | Lead-lag intra-sector momentum strategy — Pass 52 turn 69 owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED. NEW strategy class added per owner Pass 52 directive: capture cluster-effect when sector leader (e.g., INTC for semis, JPM for banks) breaks out, sympathetic moves spread to lagging peers in same correlation cluster (NVDA, AMD, etc.). Distinct from existing sector rotation (sector-level, not stock-cluster), pairs trading (mean-reversion vs lead-lag), stock-vs-sector momentum filter (precondition not strategy), and cross-asset (macro asset classes). **Substantive scope:** (1) Detection: leader breakout = high-beta-to-sector OR known industry-bellwether stock makes price move > N-day high with volume confirmation; (2) Correlation cluster: stocks in same GICS sector with rolling 90-day correlation > 0.5 to leader (uses DEC-015 methodology); (3) Entry trigger: lagging peer hasn't yet matched leader's proportional move + volume building; (4) Exit: time-stop 3-10 days OR convergence reached OR cluster move fades. **Use-case map (#57):** new strategy class for 60→70+ roster expansion; uses existing data (OHLCV + sector + correlation matrix); no new APIs required. Joint with DEC-015 (correlation methodology), DEC-367 (Pairs/Stat Arb — distinguished from lead-lag), DEC-369 (Cross-Asset — distinguished from intra-sector cluster). REVERSIBILITY: Medium — new strategy class, additive. ~3-5d engineering effort. Test signal: synthetic INTC +5% breakout day → strategy identifies NVDA/AMD/AVGO as cluster + checks lag + enters with 3-10d time stop; reference literature: Hou (2007) industries info, Cohen & Frazzini (2008) economic links predictable returns. Joint Pass 52 commit with X5 closure. **Pass 53 evening 2026-05-06 DEC-594 audit annotation: test_signal pattern keywords matched in `backtest\tests\test_e2e.py`, `backtest\tests\test_gates.py`; covered by existing pyramid (116 passed + 5 skipped). Manual verification of exact-match-vs-keyword-match queued for Day 5.** | RESOLVED-DECIDED | Batch X5 — Risk Management Extension | Pass 52 | DEC-015,DEC-367,DEC-369 |
| **DECISION-459** | AgentGateConfig spec — Option C Hybrid Architecture **Pass 52 turn 129 owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED (SUPERSEDES DEC-042):** OPTION C HYBRID ARCHITECTURE — TradingAgents Portfolio Manager native output as primary signal + separate Risk veto layer. Origin: DEC-042 turn 121 spec was executed without verifying that named agents (Bull/Bear/Risk/ChartAnalyst as parallel voters) match actual TradingAgents architecture. Owner accountability question turn 128 surfaced the gap. Re-resolution per owner directive turn 129. ARCHITECTURE: (1) PRIMARY SIGNAL — TradingAgents Portfolio Manager outputs structured Pydantic decision per propagate(): {decision: BUY/HOLD/SELL, confidence: 0.0-1.0, rationale, structured_fields...}. Confidence is consumed directly as primary gate signal (NOT re-aggregated from intermediate agent scores). (2) TIER ASSIGNMENT — confidence threshold maps to DEC-021 3-tier (HIGH ≥0.8 = 5%, MED 0.65-0.8 = 3%, LOW 0.5-0.65 = 1.5%); confidence < 0.5 → REJECT. (3) RISK VETO LAYER — separate Risk veto evaluated AFTER Portfolio Manager confidence threshold passed. Implementation options for Risk veto signal: (a) Extract Risk debate confidence from LangGraph state (preferred), (b) Run separate Risk Manager call on PM-approved candidates only (cost+latency overhead, simpler), (c) Use Portfolio Manager risk-adjusted confidence as combined signal (no separate veto layer — collapses to Option B Pure-PM-native). Default: extract from LangGraph state per (a). (4) RISK VETO LOGIC — `s_risk ≥ 0.5` hard gate; if breached → REJECT regardless of PM confidence. Owner directive #3 turn 121 carried forward: extensive A/B testing of continuous-Risk-score variants (continuous-Risk-weighted into PM confidence vs binary-veto) required REVISIT_AFTER_BACKTEST. (5) BULL/BEAR ALIGNMENT — PRESERVED from DEC-042 turn 121 spec but applied at debate-level: extract Research Manager synthesis from LangGraph state; if Research Manager indicates contested/no-consensus position (Bull-Bear debate didn't converge to high-confidence direction) → REJECT. Operationalization: Research Manager confidence ≥ 0.5 AND direction matches PM decision direction. (6) DECISION DIRECTION — uses PM decision field directly (BUY/HOLD/SELL); avoids parallel-voting interpretation issues from DEC-042. (7) OVERRIDE — Stage 2 backtest deterministic (no override); Stage 3+ owner manual override via dashboard (carried from DEC-042). A/B FRAMEWORK INTEGRATION (DEC-205-216 + DEC-211): (arm A) rules-only: bypass agents entirely; (arm B) full-agents-with-veto: PM confidence + Risk veto layer (default config); (arm C) no-Risk: Risk veto disabled (PM confidence only — collapses to Option B); (arm D) no-Bull-Bear-align: Research Manager alignment check disabled; (arm E ablation per DEC-211): per-phase weight variations on intermediate scores. JOINT WITH: DEC-021 (3-tier sizing — modifier consumer); DEC-042 (predecessor SUPERSEDED); DEC-051 (TradingAgents staged adoption — provides Portfolio Manager); DEC-058 (LLM provider strategy: GPT-5.4-mini Stage 2 backtest, Anthropic Stage 3+ live); DEC-062 (TradingAgents 5-tier → position_size_modifier — adapted: PM confidence replaces 5-tier as primary signal); DEC-131 (agent value-add ≥0.2 net Sharpe gate); DEC-205-216 (A/B cluster — arm definitions revised per Option C); DEC-211 (per-agent ablation — extracts intermediate phase scores for ablation arms). EFFORT: Sprint 7 ~2-3 days (vs DEC-042 ~1-2d): (a) AgentGateConfig dataclass with PM-native fields ~0.5d; (b) LangGraph state extraction for Risk debate + Research Manager confidence ~1d; (c) DEC-216 A/B orchestrator integration with revised arm definitions ~0.5d; (d) Risk continuous-score test infrastructure for owner directive #3 ~0.5d. TEST SIGNALS: (i) AgentGateConfig dataclass typed (confidence 0.0-1.0 invariant; Risk threshold 0.5 default; tier thresholds 0.5/0.65/0.8 default); (ii) Synthetic PM(BUY, conf=0.85) + Research Manager(align long, conf=0.7) + Risk(conf=0.6) → HIGH-tier entry (5% size); (iii) Synthetic PM(BUY, conf=0.85) + Risk(conf=0.4) → Risk veto → REJECT regardless of PM confidence; (iv) Synthetic PM(BUY, conf=0.7) + Research Manager(contested, conf=0.4) → align check fails → REJECT; (v) Synthetic PM(HOLD) → REJECT (decision direction); (vi) DEC-216 A/B orchestrator passes config per arm: full-with-veto = default; no-Risk = veto disabled; no-Bull-Bear-align = alignment disabled; (vii) Continuous-Risk A/B arm vs binary-veto arm produces measurable Sharpe delta documenting which is empirically better (REVISIT_AFTER_BACKTEST per owner directive #3 turn 121 carried forward); (viii) LangGraph state extraction unit tests verify Risk debate confidence + Research Manager confidence reachable from PM decision output. REVISIT_AFTER_BACKTEST TAGS (5): (i) Confidence threshold 0.5 — empirical tune; (ii) Tier thresholds 0.5/0.65/0.8 — empirical tune; (iii) Risk veto threshold 0.5 — empirical tune; (iv) Continuous-Risk vs binary-veto — extensive A/B per owner directive carried forward; (v) Research Manager alignment check on/off — A/B arm tests whether check adds value vs PM-only. IMPLEMENTATION OPTIONS WITHIN OPTION C (deferred to Sprint 7 implementation start): (7a) Risk signal extraction method: LangGraph state hook (recommended) vs separate Risk Manager call (cost+latency overhead) vs PM risk-adjusted confidence only (collapses to Option B); (7b) Research Manager alignment threshold: 0.5 default; (7c) Whether to log intermediate agent debate transcripts for explainability per DEC-189 + DEC-200 Dashboard 2 ICT/SMC audit. Fold 2 train 2021-05 to 2023-11 (2.5yr expanded); OOS 2023-11 to 2024-05 (6mo). Fold 3 train 2021-05 to 2024-05 (3yr expanded); OOS 2024-05 to 2024-11 (6mo). Fold 4 train 2021-05 to 2024-11 (3.5yr expanded); OOS 2024-11 to 2025-05 (6mo). Fold 5 train 2021-05 to 2025-05 (4yr expanded); OOS 2025-05 to 2025-11 (6mo). Total 5 folds × 6mo OOS = 2.5yr OOS coverage; expanding train uses ALL available history per fold; consistent with academic walk-forward methodology under data-constrained conditions. Tradeoffs vs DEC-109: smaller OOS sample (6mo vs 12mo) → wider CIs in A/B comparison; less regime variation in earliest train (2021 was post-COVID bull); more frequent retraining captures regime evolution. | **DECISION-469** | Adopt Benjamini-Hochberg FDR (q=0.10) replacing Bonferroni multi-testing correction. Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION (Cluster 1 Statistical Methodology); formally added to AUDIT_INDEX master table Pass 53 turn (this) — fixes phantom-DEC-label documentation gap. RATIONALE: Bonferroni at α=0.05 / 254,016 cube cells × 60 strategies × 6 regimes = α=3.3e-9 per test, infeasible to detect any signal. BH FDR controls expected proportion of false rejections among rejected nulls; q=0.10 means at most 10% of "PASS" cells are false positives. Less conservative than Bonferroni but appropriate for exploratory testing at scale. Resolves GAP 60 (CRITICAL — multiple-testing math doesn't reconcile). EFFORT: ~0.5d implementation in Sprint 7 statistical methodology. JOINT: DEC-470 (hierarchical structure), DEC-471 (cube reduction), DEC-110 (PSR), DEC-426 (5-Gate filter Gate 2). TEST SIGNALS: synthetic dataset with known true-positive rate produces FDR ≤ 0.10; comparison vs Bonferroni shows higher detection power. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. | PROPOSED | Sprint 7 / Statistical Methodology | Pass 52 | 53 |
| **DECISION-470** | Hierarchical 3-level FDR application (per-strategy / per-cell / per-regime). Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION; formally added to AUDIT_INDEX Pass 53 turn (this). RATIONALE: Single-level FDR across ALL cube cells loses statistical power when most cells are uninteresting. Hierarchical: Level 1 (per-strategy: 119 strategies) controls family-wise error at strategy roster level; Level 2 (per-cell within passing strategy) controls error within active strategies; Level 3 (per-regime within passing cell) controls regime-conditional inference. Each level applies BH FDR at q=0.10. Resolves GAP 60 cascade with structure. EFFORT: ~1d implementation; test infrastructure ~0.5d. JOINT: DEC-469 (BH FDR foundation), DEC-471 (cube reduction reduces hierarchy depth), DEC-426 (5-Gate filter Gate 2), DEC-487 (Phase 1A-α cube verdict consumes). TEST SIGNALS: synthetic 3-level dataset preserves expected per-level FDR; cells failing Level 1 do not advance to Level 2/3. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. | PROPOSED | Sprint 7 / Statistical Methodology | Pass 52 | 53 |
| **DECISION-471** | Cube dimensionality reduction to 8 core dimensions. Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION; formally added to AUDIT_INDEX Pass 53 turn (this). Reduce to 8 core: strategy_id × regime × sector × cap_band × vol_band × hold_band × tier (now T1a/T1b/T1c/T2/T3 per DEC-483) × smart_money_signal. Eliminated dims (days_to_earnings/days_to_FOMC/news_topic/etc.) become trade-level metadata for filtering not as cube coordinates. Cell count: ~119 strategies × 4 regimes × 11 sectors × 3 caps × 3 vols × 3 holds × 5 tiers × 2 SM = 1,178,820 → after empirical pruning ~30-50k populated cells; mean trades/cell ~0.5-2 (testable with FDR). Resolves GAP 62 (CRITICAL — cube cell sparsity / compute cost unestimated). EFFORT: ~1d cube schema refactor; ~0.5d trade-level metadata logger. | **DECISION-472** | Eliminate paired A/B design; switch to independent arms with shared opportunity set + block bootstrap CIs. Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION; formally added to AUDIT_INDEX Pass 53 turn (this). RATIONALE: Paired A/B design (same trade in arm A vs arm B) is statistically invalid when arms execute different trades (which they do — arm B with agent overlay rejects some arm A trades, takes different ones). DEC-472 mandates independent arms operating on shared opportunity set (same universe + signals); paired test invalid → use block bootstrap (10-day blocks to preserve serial correlation) on Sharpe difference / win-rate difference between arms. Confidence intervals via percentile method. Resolves GAP 92 (CRITICAL — trade set pairing statistically invalid). EFFORT: ~1.5d block bootstrap implementation; ~0.5d arm independence verification. JOINT: DEC-205-216 (A/B operational — adapted), DEC-211 (per-agent ablation), DEC-242 (distribution analysis), DEC-487 (Phase 1A-α 1-arm baseline), DEC-488 (Phase 1A-β scale-validation). TEST SIGNALS: synthetic paired vs independent comparison shows different CIs; block bootstrap CIs cover known true difference at 95% level. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. | PROPOSED | Sprint 7 / A/B Methodology | Pass 52 | 53 |

| **DECISION-496** | Tier 3 momentum watchlist methodology — Jegadeesh-Titman 12-1 month price momentum (academic standard) computed via SCREENER-FIRST two-step flow against the broad US equities market. **Pass 53 owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED** with classic defaults: lookback=252 / skip=21 / risk-adjustment=OFF / tie-breakers=(volatility ascending → ADV descending). **Pass 53 owner-corrected SCREENER-FIRST architecture (this body update post initial DEC-496 approval): T3 must be a market screener, not a re-rank of existing T1 cache.** Sprint placement: Sprint 1 historical populate via market-wide monthly screener for full 2020-2026 backfill; Sprint 5 phased automation per DEC-104/375/376/377 for ongoing monthly refresh. RATIONALE: DEC-104 / DEC-364 specified "auto-populate Tier 3 momentum watchlist" without defining "momentum"; initial DEC-496 spec defined J-T 12-1 methodology but implicitly assumed T1 cache as input universe — owner caught the conceptual gap: "If we run tier 3 calculations on sp500, how are we serving the purpose. Needs to be a market screener and from the tickers identified we will need to pull polygon end points." Top 100 NON-T1 momentum names cannot be identified by ranking only T1 tickers. CORRECTED TWO-STEP FLOW: **Step 1 (Screener):** at each monthly snapshot date D, fetch Polygon `/v2/aggs/grouped/locale/us/market/stocks/{date}` daily-bar grouped endpoint for D-252 through D-21 (covers full lookback window across all ~6000-8000 listed US equities including ADRs); filter to common stock + active per Polygon `/v3/reference/tickers` metadata; apply DEC-321/366 Tier 3 liquidity floor (min_cap=$300M, min_avg_dollar_volume=$5M, min_history=60d); compute `momentum_score = (price[D-21] / price[D-252]) - 1` per ticker; exclude T1a/T1b/T1c sub-tier members at as_of D; rank descending; take top 100 = Tier 3 monthly snapshot. **Step 2 (Prefetch):** the 100 identified non-T1 tickers (union across all 72 monthly snapshots in 2020-2026 backtest window — expected 500-1000 unique tickers) get OHLCV-prefetched to local cache via standard Polygon `/v2/aggs/ticker/{ticker}/range/...` calls used for T1; downstream backtest runs against prefetched cache. MARKET-STANDARD METHODOLOGY (unchanged): `momentum_score = (price[D-21 trading days] / price[D-252 trading days]) - 1`. Skip recent 21 trading days (~1 month) to avoid short-term mean reversion. Lookback 252 trading days (~12 months). Risk-adjustment OFF (classic Jegadeesh-Titman). UNIVERSE FOR SCREENING (REVISED): broad US equities market (Polygon grouped endpoint), NOT existing T1 cache. **PIT CORRECTNESS:** each Tier 3 entry has `added_date` (first month rank ≤100) and `removed_date` (first month rank >100); B++ schema same as T1a/T1c. UPDATE TIMING: Step 1 screener runs FIRST (months 0-3 of Sprint 1 historical populate); Step 2 prefetch runs immediately after (~1 month based on union ticker count). TIE-BREAKERS: secondary rank by 6-month volatility ascending (lower-vol momentum preferred); tertiary by ADV descending (more liquid preferred). HISTORICAL SCOPE (Pass 53 owner-approved C: full historical scope): full 2020-2026 monthly snapshots = ~72 monthly compute cycles; expected union universe 500-1000 unique non-T1 tickers; cache size implication ~2-3× larger than T1-only. Joint: DEC-104 (parent), DEC-364 (size 100), DEC-321/366 (T3 liquidity floor: $300M cap, $5M ADV, 60d history), DEC-375/376/377 (Sprint 5 phased implementation), DEC-380 (Polygon corp actions parent for screener pipeline), L143 (this body update is forward-looking spec correction in-window, not history rewrite). TEST SIGNALS: (a) screener fn returns ≥100 non-T1 tickers ranked deterministically for synthetic dataset with known returns; (b) PIT loader returns correct monthly top 100 for any as_of D in 2020-2026; (c) two-step flow integration test: Step 1 screener → Step 2 prefetch → backtest reads cache; (d) monthly refresh handles list churn (entrants + exits) without orphan tickers. EFFORT REVISED Pass 53 owner-approved (Q-B): Sprint 1 ~28-39d → ~35-50d (+~7-11d for screener + monthly compute + ticker-union prefetch). Owner approved screener-first architecture + full 2020-2026 historical scope + Sprint 1 effort revision Pass 53. **Pass 53 evening 2026-05-06 DEC-594 audit: PARTIAL-SPEC-ONLY (was RESOLVED-DECIDED; demoted via DEC-594 retroactive audit Day 4-5 — code-grep on test_signal keywords found no match in `backtest/tests/`; cannot advance to RESOLVED-DECIDED until executable artifact lands per DEC-594).** | PARTIAL-SPEC-ONLY | Sprint 1 (screener + historical populate, ~35-50d revised) + Sprint 5 (ongoing automation) | Pass 53 | 53 |

| **DECISION-503** | **Comprehensive test pyramid before every code push (HARD RULE).** Pass 53 owner directive 2026-05-05 RESOLVED-DECIDED. RULE: every code push (new feature / bug fix / refactor / schema change / data-source migration) must execute and pass ALL applicable test types: (1) Unit — individual function correctness with mocked dependencies; (2) Smoke — basic happy-path verification on real data ≤30s; (3) Integration — module-to-module data flow (e.g., fetcher → cache → signals → screener); (4) System — end-to-end workflow (full prefetch → universe load → backtest → report); (5) Functional — feature behavior matches spec; (6) Regression — full `backtest/tests/test_unit.py` + `backtest/tests/test_integration.py` (36/36 must pass); (7) Data integrity — schema validation, PIT semantics, completeness gates; (8) Performance / load — for prefetch + heavy-data code (rate-limit handling, memory bounds, wall-time budgets); (9) Acceptance — owner-defined pass criteria for the specific change. Partial coverage ("just unit tests" or "just smoke + integration") is non-compliant. If a test type doesn't apply (e.g., performance for a typo fix), explicitly mark N/A with reason. RATIONALE: silent-gap finding (BUG-271/272/273 — smart_money.py has been silently broken for 3 of 4 Quiver endpoints) went undetected because tests focused on `congresstrading` (which works) and skipped `insidertrading` + `institutionalholdings` + `analystestimates`. Comprehensive coverage would have caught this. Owner directive 2026-05-05: "Just dont do a few limited tests." TRIGGER: ANY code push — including doc-only repos that touch executable scripts. APPLY: codified CHECKLIST.md item #69 + CLAUDE.md HARD RULE Pass 53 (this turn). FIRST APPLICATION: smart_money silent-gap fix (BUG-271/272/273) next turn — full test pyramid required. JOINT: DEC-097 (90% test coverage minimum), DEC-098 (hot-path 100% coverage), CHECKLIST #69 (this DEC codifies), L145 (silent-gap lesson). | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Cross-cutting / process | Pass 53 | 53 |
| **DECISION-508** | **Smartmoneyconcepts fork extensive testing protocol (15-category plan + 3-phase A/B/C gate).** Pass 53 owner-approved Q1+Q2+Q3 2026-05-05 RESOLVED-DECIDED. RULE: Any external library forked under DEC-045 (or future fork-first DECs) must complete the 15-category test plan + 3-phase gate before strategies consume its signals. CHECKLIST #71 codifies the rule. Q1 approve protocol; Q2 start Phase A testing NOW (not wait Sprint 8); Q3 owner does manual Dashboard 2 validation review. RATIONALE: ICT/SMC signals are pattern-based with subjective ground truth — 4 risks: (1) lookahead bias (library may compute swing high using future bars); (2) pattern-matching noise (algorithms find FVGs in random data); (3) subjective ground truth (no canonical right answer; need pinned-version reproducibility); (4) performance scale (1937 tickers × 1000 days × multiple primitives). FIFTEEN TEST CATEGORIES across 4 tiers: Tier 1 (correctness; must-pass before merge): unit tests on synthetic OHLCV (~50-100 tests), canonical pattern fixtures, **PIT correctness regression CRITICAL** (freezegun: signal at bar D unchanged when computed at D+50 per DEC-261 N+1 lag), edge case handling, library version pin + reproducibility. Tier 2 (integration; must-pass before strategy enable): cache pipeline integration, cross-primitive composition, survivorship + corporate actions, performance/load. Tier 3 (empirical validation; must-pass before backtest): statistical sanity (signal frequency bounds), adversarial random-walk test (library on Brownian motion ~ baseline rate not pattern-matching), cross-validation against known source (TradingView ICT script), signal lookahead detection (>65% win rate flag per DEC-084). Tier 4 (visual + manual): Dashboard 2 (DEC-200; existing Pass 52 turn 79 5-section spec), owner manual spot-check ~20-30 signals. THREE-PHASE GATE: Phase A (PRE-MERGE; library in vendored/, not main): all Tier 1+2+3 tests pass; ≥90% line coverage; library NOT imported outside test files; OWNER REVIEW + APPROVAL → Phase B. Phase B (CANARY; library imported, strategies disabled): signals computed for full universe → data_prefetch/ict_smc/{ticker}.parquet but strategies don't consume; Dashboard 2 launched; owner manual validates 20-50 signals; statistical sanity report; PIT regression on full universe; OWNER REVIEW + APPROVAL → Phase C. Phase C (PRODUCTION; strategies enabled): ICT/SMC Layer 2 strategies (per STRATEGY_REGISTER) consume signals; backtest run; A/B vs baseline comparison; DEC-084 red-flag check (>65% win rate = lookahead suspect); walk-forward validation per DEC-505 4 OOS folds; integration accepted. EFFORT: ~15-23d engineering + variable owner review (Tier 1 ~5-7d / Tier 2 ~2-3d / Tier 3 ~2d / Dashboard 2 ~3-4d / canary + owner review variable / production backtest ~1-2d). TIMING per Q2: Phase A starts NOW (Pass 53 Sprint 0A); not waiting Sprint 8. Q3: owner does manual review (no paid reviewer delegation). JOINT: DEC-045 (fork-first parent), DEC-200 (Dashboard 2), DEC-261 (PIT N+1 lag), DEC-084 (>65% win rate red flag), DEC-503 (test pyramid; Tier 1-3 USE pyramid layers), DEC-507 (wiring matrix; complementary), L147 (external library fork integration risk lesson; this DEC is first instantiation), CHECKLIST #71 (codifies this protocol). FIRST APPLICATION: smartmoneyconcepts library Phase A testing kicks off Pass 53 Sprint 0A as Batch 15. PATTERN REUSABLE: future forks (TradingAgents v0.2.4, future libraries) follow same 15-category + 3-phase protocol. | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Cross-cutting / process | Pass 53 | 53 |

| **DECISION-598** | **Pyramid cell 4-state semantics + per-ID override mechanism.** Pass 53 v8h+1 owner-approved 2026-05-09 RESOLVED-DECIDED. CHANGE: pyramid cells extended from boolean YES/no to 4-state YES / no / N/A / LATER:reason. SEMANTICS: YES = test in this layer references the ID; no = layer applies but no coverage (real gap); N/A = layer doesn't apply to this ID type (e.g. property-based for env-guard fix); LATER:reason = applicable but deferred (hover tooltip). MECHANISM: `PYRAMID_OVERRIDES` dict in `scripts/build_dashboard_stage_2.py` declares per-ID per-layer applicability. Detected coverage (YES) wins; otherwise override or default false applies. RATIONALE: BUG-007 (env-guard fix; only `regression` layer applies) was rendering 12 layers as "no" — misleading. N/A annotation surfaces correctness honestly. | **DECISION-504** | **T3-over-T1 multi-tier precedence rule (universe membership resolver).** Pass 53 owner directive 2026-05-05 RESOLVED-DECIDED ("If a ticker is in multiple tiers, rules of T3 should apply over T1"). PRECEDENCE ORDER (most-specific wins): T3 (momentum) > T2 (spinoff/IPO) > T1c (NDX-non-S&P) > T1a (S&P 500) > T1ETF. SCOPE owner Q1=Approve all (a)-(e): (a) Liquidity floor — T3=$5M ADV vs T1=$10M; T3 applies for dual T1+T3 tickers; (b) History minimum — T3=60d vs T1=250d; T3 applies; (c) Position sizing — tier-specific tier→size map; T3 sizing applies; (d) Strategy roster — T3-eligible strategies fire for resolved tier; (e) Refresh cadence — T3 monthly vs T1 quarterly (ops-level). RATIONALE: T3 captures NON-T1 momentum names; if a ticker graduates into T1 from T3 (rare; e.g., VST May 2024), the T3 momentum thesis is more recent + more specific. T3 rules should apply because T3 was the reason the ticker was screened. CANONICAL EXAMPLE: VST joined S&P 500 2024-05-08; T3 had it as momentum top-100 with added_date=2024-05-01, removed_date=2024-06-03. For as_of=2024-06-01, both T1a and T3 active → resolves to T3. For as_of=2024-07-01, T3 removed → resolves to T1a. IMPLEMENTATION: `backtest/data/universe.py` — `_TIER_PRECEDENCE` constant + `TIER_PARAMS` dict (per-tier liquidity/history/sizing/cadence) + `resolve_tier_precedence(ticker, as_of)` function returns most-specific tier name; `get_tier_params(ticker, as_of)` returns parameter dict for resolved tier. Master Dedup CSV gets new `resolved_tier` column (post-build). TEST SIGNALS (10 unit tests, all PASS): tier order = T3>T2>T1c>T1a>T1ETF; TIER_PARAMS complete with 5 required keys; T3 more permissive than T1a (ADV + history); VST 2024-06-01→T3, VST 2024-07-01→T1a; T2∩T3→T3; T1a-only→T1a; SPY→T1ETF; unknown ticker→None; get_tier_params returns mutation-safe copy. JOINT: DEC-040 (PointInTimeLoader contract), DEC-321/366 (T3 liquidity floor), DEC-103 (T2 thresholds), DEC-477 (T1a canonical), DEC-483 (T1c sub-tier), DEC-104 (T3 momentum methodology), CHECKLIST #69 (test pyramid first application). FIRST APPLICATION: this turn — DEC-504 implementation went through full test pyramid (10 new unit tests + integration + regression 79/79 + smoke + functional + acceptance). | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 53 / Sprint 0A | Pass 53 | 53 |
